#### 重要提示

公司董事会及董事、高级管理人员保证本报告及摘要所载资料不存在任何虚假记载、误导性陈述或者重大遗漏，并对其内容的真实性、准确性和完整性承担个别及连带责任。

公司年度财务报告已经中兴华会计师事务所（特殊普通合伙）根据财政部颁布的《企业会计准则（2006）》审计，并出具了标准无保留意见的审计报告。

本年度报告及摘要中比较列报的以前年度财务数据已经中兴华会计师事务所（特殊普通合伙）根据财政部颁布的《企业会计准则（2006）》进行审计调整。

本年度报告中的财务数据为合并报表经审计后的数据，资本相关数据及主要经营指标按照监管要求合并报表口径计算得出。

公司董事长岳隆杰、行长朱鸣以及财务负责人刘海新，保证年度报告中财务报告的真实、完整。

#### IMPORTANT NOTES

The Board of Directors, the directors and senior management of the Company undertake that there is no misrepresentation, misleading statement, or material omission in the Report and its summary, and accept joint and several liabilities for the authenticity, accuracy and completeness of the contents of the Report.

The annual financial report of the Company has been audited by Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP according to the Accounting Standards for Business Enterprises (2006) promulgated by the Ministry of Finance and a standard unqualified auditor's opinion has been issued.

The financial data of previous years listed in this annual report and its summary for comparison have been audited and adjusted by Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP according to the Accounting Standards for Business Enterprises (2006) promulgated by the Ministry of Finance.

The financial data listed in this annual report is the audited data of consolidated statements and capital related data has been calculated upon the standard of consolidated statements in accordance with the regulatory requirements.

Chairman Yue Longjie, President Zhu Ming and Finance Director Liu Haixin confirm the authenticity and completeness of the financial statements in the Annual Report.

## 九、公司各类风险和风险管理情况

公司全面风险管理体系由董事会、监事会、高级管理层、全面风险管理部门、风险管理主控部门、风险管理责任部门、内审部门及各分支机构构成，并建立了包括政策制度、管理办法、操作细则、规范性文件在内的四层级制度体系。公司董事会负责建立和保持有效的全面风险管理体系，承担风险管理的最终责任。高级管理层负责制定风险管理政策和程序，承担全面风险管理的实施责任。高级管理层下设信用风险审查委员会、资产负债管理委员会等专业委员会，并指定各类风险主控部门及管理部门。针对信用风险、市场风险、操作风险等各类风险，公司制定相应的风险管理政策及程序，建设上线信用风险、操作风险、流动性风险等管理信息系统，明确了各类风险识别、计量、监测、缓释及报告流程，确保各类风险能够准确识别、有效处置。其中，公司采用权重法计量信用风险加权资产及资本要求，采用标准法计量市场风险加权资产及资本要求，采用基本指标法计量操作风险加权资产及资本要求。

本要求。2019年，公司始终坚持高质量稳健发展为中心，不断健全全面风险管理框架体系，持续完善风险管理制度流程，扎实推动各项风控措施落地落细，基本实现业务产品全面覆盖、制度流程全程控制、风险防控全员参与，保障了各项业务持续稳健发展。

## (一) 信用风险及对策

信用风险是指债务人或交易对手未能履行合同规定的义务或信用质量发生变化，影响金融产品价值，从而给公司造成经济损失的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强信用风险管理：一是持续推进信贷精细化管理。修订《莱商银行法人客户统一授信管理办法》等制度办法，制定授信调查统一作业模板和标准，健全信贷业务全流程管控机制，实现信贷业务前中后台部门分设、职责分离、相互制衡。二是强化信贷业务全流程管控。制定年度授信政策指引，建立实施授信项目延伸审查、限时办结和后评估机制，修订完善放款审核管理办法和操作规程，全面推行总、分行主贷后管理人模式，努力推动贷款“三查”措施落地落实，严控新增授信风险。三是调整优化信贷业务结构。制定大额授信压降计划，调整优化信贷资源配置、信用风险评估、激励约束考核等机制，合理规划中长期贷款规模、期限及还款方式，重点加强民营企业、小微企业金融服务能力，有效降低授信集中度风险。四是加大存量风险处置力度。不断完善存量风险资产处置化解工作机制，强化不良资产台账式管理，严格落实风险处置计划与措施，加快存量风险处置化解步伐。

## （二）市场风险及对策

市场风险是指因市场价格（利率、汇率、股票价格和商品价格）的不利变动，从而使银行表内外业务发生损失的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强市场风险管理：一是强化指标监测预警。逐日监控市场风险限额执行及市场信用违约情况，按季监测利率敏感性指标，及时采取措施消除风险隐患。二是加大资金业务风险管控。根据全行资产负债规划，合理配置投资资产种类及期限结构，提升短期资金利用率及收益水平，规避市场波动带来的利率风险。三是加强银行账簿利率风险管理。依托资产负债管理系统，建设完成银行账簿利率风险的计量体系，实现银行账簿利率风险计量的独立审查和验证。同时定期开展银行账户利率风险压力测试，根据压力测试结果，适时适当调整优化未来利率期限结构。四是规范外汇业务流程管控。科学制定外汇业务操作规程，推行外汇资金交易分级授权管理，强化资金交易员权限限制。同时严格执行代客结售汇业务平盘制度，对代客大额结售汇业务逐笔平盘，降低汇率波动带来的汇率损失。

## (三)操作风险及对策

操作风险是指由不完善或有问题的内部控制程序、员工、信息科技系统以及外部事件所造成损失的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强操作风险管理：一是组织开展各类监督检查。制定年度合规检查与风险排查计划35项，组织开展全行性业务监督检查工作，全面梳理排查操作风险隐患，完善

各项制度流程，加大问题整改和问责力度，有效规范员工操作行为，保障各项业务合规有序开展。二是组织开展“巩固治乱象成果”“行业规范建设年”工作，对照监管要求，对历次内外部检查、深化整治乱象、飞行检查等发现的问题进行“回头看”检查，对道德规范、行为规范、服务规范、管理规范等进行梳理总结和补充。三是持续增强员工行为管理。定期开展员工行为排查，及时排除风险隐患。同时组织开展案防合规知识学习测试，组织全员分批次参加考核，进一步提升全员自律意识、案防意识、合规意识。四是全力构筑法律风险防线。以持续强化法律服务支撑为基础，扎实推进格式合同电子化、非格式合同检查、法律知识培训等工作，建立健全内外部律师管理制度流程，严格防范法律风险。

## (四)流动性风险及对策

流动性风险是指公司无法以合理成本及时获得充足资金，用于偿付到期债务、履行其他支付义务和满足正常业务开展的其他资金需求的风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强流动性风险管理：一是强化流动性监测预测。加强资金缺口分析预测，做好流动性指标动态计量与监测，全面提升流动性风险识别及预警效率，有效防范流动性风险。二是优化资产负债期限结构。负债端，不断加大存款营销力度，持续推动存款快速增长，降低同业融资比例；合理设置FTP定价策略，引导分支机构加大营销中长期存款积极性。资产端，加大资产到期期限管理，合理确定新增贷款到期期限分布，适当控制长期投资业务，促进月度资金流入均衡平稳，有效降低资产负债期限错配。三是增持优质流动性资产。通过减持企业债、政策性金融债和增持国债等措施，扩大优质流动性资产储备规模，保障优质流动性资产充足率持续达标。四是强化风险应对能力。组织开展流动性压力测试，合理设置测试情景，充分揭示潜在流动性风险，进一步修订完善流动性风险管控措施。组织开展流动性应急演练，重点模拟挤兑等极端情形下，总分支机构联动处置能力，进一步提升流动性风险应对能力。

## （五）信息科技风险及对策

信息科技风险是指信息科技在银行运用过程中，由于自然因素、人为因素、技术漏洞和管理缺陷产生的操作、法律和声誉等风险。报告期内，公司从以下方面加强信息科技风险管理：一是完善信息科技治理体系。组织召开信息科技管理委员会会议，审议通过2019年度信息科技预算等18项议案，修订完善《莱商银行信息系统安全管理办法》等44项制度，努力构建信息科技风险防范长效治理机制。二是推进信息科技基础设施建设。不断加大基础设施投入，顺利完成UPS功率模块扩容、域名解析服务器部署、数据中心机房改造等工作，并建成启用总行生产运维操作室，信息科技风控基础不断夯实。三是增强网络安全管理能力。扎实开展内网安全管理系统升级、网络防火墙体系建设等工作，分步骤对全行无线设备进行风险排查和问题整改，信息网络安全水平显著提升。四是强化信息安全保护。顺利完成六期基础软硬件产品缺陷信息协查、重要信息系统等保定级、数据库审计系统升级等工

作，信息安全保护能力逐步提升。五是提升业务连续性管理能力。在做好灾备机房、UPS设备日常巡检的基础上，组织开展业务系统切换演练、消防系统演练等22次，启动同城灾备中心建设，并上线运行数据备份系统，业务连续性管理能力进一步增强。

## 十、内部控制评价

公司高度重视内部控制建设，遵循全覆盖、制衡性、审慎性及相匹配的内控原则，建立健全内部控制管理体系，不断深化内控管理体制机制改革，持续完善内控管理制度流程，全面贯彻落实各项内控管理措施。通过对内部控制体系建设、实施和运行结果开展系统性调查、测试、分析和评估，公司内部控制体系从内部控制环境、风险识别与评估、内部控制措施与执行、内部检查监督、信息交流与反馈等方面，基本涵盖了所有业务、产品、渠道、管理及其关键环节等内控要素，内部控制体系基本健全，流程设置科学合理，在强化公司治理、推动转型发展、创新业务产品、防范化解风险等方面得到了有效的执行。

## 十一、内部审计情况

公司设立内审部为内部审计机构，负责对公司所有的业务和经营管理活动、经营状况进行综合审计评价。内审部对董事会及其审计委员会负责，内审工作由监事长分管，内审部分别向董事会和监事会报告审计结果，审计工作具有独立性。

报告期内，公司围绕发展战略和中心任务，严格落实行业监管要求，坚持以问题和风险为导向，以防案控险为目标，合理拟订年度审计计划，紧盯重点机构、重点领域、重点岗位和重要环节，组织实施对全行各项业务活动、经营情况及重要岗位人员任期经济责任的审计，监督、评价法律、法规和规章制度的执行情况，向管理层提出改进建议，督促、跟进整改落实，积极发挥服务监督职能，推动全行制度、流程、系统持续完善，保障业务合规、稳健发展。

## IX. Various Risks and Risk Management of the Company

The Company's comprehensive risk management system is composed of the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors, senior management, the Comprehensive Risk Management Department, the Main Risk Management Control Department, the Risk Management Responsibility Department, the Internal Audit Department and their branches, and has established a four-level system including policy system, management measures, operation rules and normative documents. The Board of Directors is responsible for establishing and maintaining an effective and comprehensive risk management system and ultimately for risk management. The senior management is responsible for formulating the risk management policies.

and procedures and for implementing comprehensive risk management. The senior management has set up special committees such as the Credit Risk Review Committee and the Assets-Liabilities Management Committee, and also designated the main control departments and management departments of various risks. The company has formulated the corresponding risk management policies and procedures for various risks such as credit risk, market risk and operation risk, built an online management information system for credit risk, operation risk and liquidity risk, and specified various types of risk identification, measurement, monitoring, controlled release and reporting processes to ensure that various risks can be accurately identified and effectively disposed. That is, the company uses the weighting method to measure credit risk weighted assets and capital requirements, the standard method to measure market risk weighted assets and capital requirements, and the basic index method to measure operation risk weighted assets and capital requirements. In 2019, the Company always focused on high-quality and sound development, continuously improved the comprehensive risk management framework system, sustainably perfected the process of the risk management system, and solidly promoted the finest implementation of various risk control measures, thus basically realizing the complete coverage of products, full-process control of system and process and all-staff participation of risk prevention and control and ensuring the continuous and steady development of various businesses.

#### (i) Credit Risks and Their Countermeasures

Credit risk refers to the risks of economic losses to the Company caused by the influence on the value of financial products resulted from failure to fulfill the obligations stipulated in the contract of or changes of the credit quality of debtors or counterparties. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened credit risk management from the following aspects: First, to continuously promote the elaborate management for credit; The Company revised the system method such as Laishang Bank Corporate Customers Unified Credit Management Approach, formulated the unified job template and standard for credit investigation, perfected the full-process management and control mechanism for credit services, and realized the distributed setup, segregation of duties and mutual counterbalance of front, medium and background credit departments. Second, to strengthen the full-process management and control for credit services; The Company formulated the annual credit policy guidance, established and implemented the extended review for credit projects, time-limited handling and post-evaluation mechanism, revised and improved the management measures and operation procedures for loan review, fully promoted the manager mode for the main loan of the headquarter and branches, and strived to drive the implementation of the "three-check" measures and strictly controlled the newly added credit risk. Third, to adjust and optimize the credit business structure; The Company formulated the large credit pressure drop plan, adjusted and optimized mechanisms such as credit resource allocation, credit risk assessment and incentive constraint evaluation, reasonably planned the scope of medium and long-term loans, time limit and payment method, focused on strengthening the financial service ability of private enterprises and small and micro businesses and effectively reduced the credit concentration risk. Fourth, to intensify stock risk disposal; The Company continuously improved the working mechanism for stock risk asset disposal, strengthened the parameter-type management for non-performing assets, strictly implemented the risk disposal plan and measurement, and accelerated the procedure of stock risk disposal.

#### (ii) Market Risks and Their Countermeasures

Market risk refers to the risks of losses of in-balance-sheet and off-balance-sheet businesses due to

adverse changes in market price (interest rate, exchange rate, stock price and commodity price). During the reporting period, the Company strengthened market risk management from the following aspects: First, to intensify monitoring and early warning for indicators; The Company monitored the implementation of market risk limits and market credit default every day and monitored the indicator of interest rate sensitivity quarterly and took measures to eliminate the potential risks in time. Second, to strengthen the management and control of fund business risk; The Company, according to the assets and liabilities plan throughout the Bank, reasonably allocated the types of investment assets and term structure, increased the short-term funds utilization and income level and avoided the interest risk arising out of market fluctuations. Third, to strengthen the management of interest rate risk of bank accounts; Relying on the asset liability management system, the Company completed to establish the measuring system for interest rate risk of bank accounts, and realized the independent review and validation of interest rate risk measurement of bank accounts, while regularly carrying out the interest rate risk stress tests for bank accounts and appropriately adjusting and optimizing the term structure of future interest rates in a timely manner based on the results of risk stress tests. Fourth, to standardize management and process of foreign businesses; The Company scientifically formulated the operating specification for foreign businesses, promoted hierarchical authorization for foreign exchange fund transactions and strengthened the authorization limit of foreign exchange dealers, while strictly implementing the settlement system for settlement and sale of foreign exchange on behalf of customers, and trading on the foreign exchange market for each large amount foreign exchange settlement and sales, to reduce the exchange rate risk caused by exchange rate fluctuation.

##### (iii) Operational Risks and Their Countermeasures

Operational risk refers to the risks of loss caused by imperfect or problematic internal control procedures, employees, IT systems, or external events. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened operational risk management from the following aspects: First, to organize and carry out various types of supervision and inspection; The Company formulated 35 annual compliance check and risk investigation plans, organized and carried out supervision and inspection of the business throughout the Bank, fully teased and investigated potential operating risks, improved various system processes, strengthened rectification accountability efforts for problems, effectively standardized the operation behavior of employees, and ensured the compliant and orderly proceeding of various businesses. Second, to organize and carry out "consolidating the result of chaos governance" and the "industry standard construction year", refer to the monitoring requirement, conduct "follow-up" inspection for problems found in all internal and external inspection, deepening chaos governance and flight check, and tease out, summarize and complement the moral norm, behavior norm, service norm and management norm. Third, to continuously enhance the management of employee behavior; The Company regularly carried out employee behavior investigation and eliminated potential risks in time, while organizing and carrying out the knowledge learning test of case prevention compliance, organizing all staffs to participate in assessment in batches, and further increasing the self-discipline consciousness, case prevention consciousness and compliance consciousness of all staffs. Fourth, to spare no effort to build the defensive line for legal risks; Based on continuously strengthening support of legal services, the Company solidly promoted the electronization of standard contract, non-standard contract check and law knowledge training, established and perfected the management system process for internal and external lawyers, and strictly prevented legal risks.

##### (iv) Liquidity Risks and Their Countermeasures

Liquidity risk refers to the risks of inability to timely obtain sufficient funds at a reasonable cost for the purpose of repaying due debts, fulfilling other payment obligations or meeting other needs for funds used for normal business. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened liquidity risk management from the following aspects: First, to strengthen the forecast for liquidity monitoring. The Company intensified the analysis and forecast for financial gap, dynamically measured and monitored liquidity indexes, fully enhanced the liquidity risk identification and early warning efficiency to effectively prevent liquidity risks. Second, to optimize the term structure of assets and liabilities; for the liabilities side, the Company continuously reinforced deposit marketing, sustainably promoted the rapid growth of deposits and reduced the financing proportion from banks; The Company reasonably set up the FTP pricing strategy, and guided branches to increase the initiative of medium and long-term deposits. For the assets side, the Company reinforced the management of asset maturity, reasonably determined the distribution of newly added loan maturity, properly controlled long-term investment, promoted the balancing and stabilization of monthly capital inflows, and effectively reduced the maturity mispairing of assets and liabilities. Third, to increase high quality liquidity assets; The Company expanded the reserve scale of high quality liquidity assets by reducing corporate liabilities, policy-based financial liabilities and increasing government bonds, to ensure the sustainable compliance of the adequacy ratio of high quality liquidity assets. Fourth, to intensify risk response; The Company organized and carried out the liquidity pressure test, reasonably set up test scenarios, fully revealed the potential liquidity risk, and further revised and improved the management and control of liquidity risks. The Company organized and carried out liquidity emergency drill, focused on simulating the interconnected disposal between the headquarters and branches under extreme circumstances such as bank run to further enhance the liquidity risk response.

##### (v) IT Risks and Their Countermeasures

Information technology (IT) risk refers to the operational, legal and reputation risks generated due to natural factors, human factors, technical loopholes and management defects in the application process of information technology in banks. During the reporting period, the Company strengthened IT risk management from the following aspects: First, to perfect the IT governance system. The Company organized and held the conference of the IT management committee on which 18 proposals including 2019 IT budget were passed and 44 systems including Laishang Bank Information System Security Management Measures were revised and improved, and strived to build the long-term prevention and governance system for IT risks. Second, to drive the construction of IT infrastructures; The Company continuously increased the investment of infrastructures, smoothly completed the capacity expansion of UPS power modules, deployment of the domain name server and renovation for the computer room in the data center, and established and launched the production O&M operation room for the headquarter to continuously solidify the foundation for IT risk prevention. Third, to improve the network security management capability; The Company solidly carried out upgrade for the Intranet security management system and construction for the network firewall system, and conducted risk investigation and problem rectification for wireless devices throughout the Bank step by step to significantly enhance the IT network security level. Fourth, to intensify information security protection; The Company successfully completed six-phase basic hardware and software product defect information investigation, grading of the important

information system and database audit system upgrade to gradually improve the information security protection capacity. Fifth, to enhance the business continuity management capacity; On the basis of routine inspection of emergency room and UPS equipment, the Company organized and carried out 22 business system switchover drills and firefighting system drills, etc., launched the construction of the emergency center in the same city, and run the data backup system online to further enhance the business continuity management capability.

## X. Evaluation of Internal Control

The Company attached great importance to the construction of internal control, followed the matched principle of internal control with full coverage, counterbalance and prudence, established and improved the internal control management system, continuously deepened the reform of the internal control management system mechanism, continuously improved the internal control management system process, and fully implemented various internal control management measures. Through systematic investigation, test, analysis and evaluation of the construction, implementation and operation results of the internal control system, the Company's internal control system basically covers all internal control elements such as business, product, channel, management and their key links from the aspects of internal control environment, risk identification and evaluation, internal control measures and implementation, internal inspection and supervision, information exchange and feedback, etc. The internal control system is basically sound, with scientifically reasonable process, and has been effectively implemented in strengthening corporate governance, promoting transformation and development, innovating business products, and preventing and dissolving risks, etc.

## XI. Internal Audit

The internal audit department is set up in the Bank, which is responsible for comprehensive audit and evaluation for all of its business and operation management, and operating conditions. The internal audit department is responsible for the Board of Directors and its audit committee, and internal audit is in charge of the supervisor. The internal audit department shall report the audit results respectively to the Board of Directors and the Board of Supervisors, which is independent.

During the reporting period, the Bank focused on the development strategy and central tasks, strictly implemented the industry regulation requirements, adhered to regard problems and risks as the orientation and risk prevention and control as the goal, reasonably drafted the annual audit plan, closely followed key institutions, key areas, key posts and important links, organized and carried out the audit of all business activities, operation conditions and the economic responsibility of the personnel in important positions throughout the Bank in their terms of office, supervised and evaluated the implementation of laws, rules and regulations, posed suggestions for improvement to the management, urged and followed up the implementation of rectification, actively played the function of service supervision, promoted the continuous improvement of the whole institution, process and system throughout the Bank, and ensured business compliant and healthy development.

## 四、关联交易情况

根据中国银保监会、企业会计准则关联交易管理的有关规定，公司按不同监管要求对关联方的不同定义，分类识别与确认关联方，定期更新确定关联方名单。

公司与关联方发生关联交易业务均系公司正常经营活动需要，交易条件及定价水平均执行公司业务管理和监管机构的一般规定，不存在优于一般借款人或交易对手的情形，业务程序亦符合监管机构及公司关联交易管理的相关规定。

报告期内，公司的关联交易主要为对关联方提供的表内外信贷业务及与关联方发生的资金业务往来，全部关联交易均正常履约，未对公司的经营成果和财务状况产生负面影响。

### IV. The Related Transactions

In accordance with the relevant provisions of China Banking and Insurance Regulatory Commission and the related transaction management of the enterprise accounting standards, the Company, in accordance with different definitions of the related parties based on different regulatory requirements, classified and identified the related parties and regularly updated and identified the list of related parties.

The related transaction business between the Company and related parties is the normal business requirement of the Company, and the general provisions of the Company's business management and regulatory agencies are implemented for the transaction conditions and pricing levels, without any circumstance better than the general borrower or counterparty. The business procedures also comply with the related provisions of the regulatory authorities and the Company's related transaction management.

During the reporting period, the Company's related transactions are mainly in-balance-sheet and off-balance-sheet credit businesses provided for the related party and the capital transactions occurring in the related party. All related transactions are performed normally, without any negative effect on the Company's operating results and financial conditions.

## (IV) Implementation of Regulatory Provisions

At the end of the reporting period, the maximum credit granting balance for a single affiliated party was RMB 0.92 billion, accounting for 7.86% of the total net capital of the Bank; that for a single related legal person or group client to which the other organization belongs was RMB 1.365 billion, accounting for 11.66% of the total net capital of the Bank; the total credit granting balance for all affiliated parties was RMB 4.177 billion, accounting for 35.68% of the total net capital of the Bank, all of which met the regulatory requirements.

## （四）董事、监事、高级管理人员变动情况

2019年10月，因董事会换届，董立志、栾斌、王永胜、王建章不再担任董事，选举陈肖鸿、张文、段伟为第五届董事会董事；因监事会换届，胡天晓、许英强、陈起业不再担任监事，选举张小艾、申凤玲、栾斌为第五届监事会监事；聘任冯现刚、李锋为副行长，魏汝浩为首席信息官。

## （六）公司薪酬架构情况

### 1. 薪酬管理架构及决策程序

公司建立了科学的薪酬管理架构，明确规定董事、监事的报酬由股东大会决定，董事会决定高级管理人员的薪酬。董事会对公司薪酬管理负最终责任，负责审阅、批准、修订公司薪酬管理制度与政策、薪酬成本与预算等，监督公司薪酬制度实施情况，并定期评估公司薪酬机制的健全性和有效性。董事会下设提名与薪酬考核委员会，负责审议有关薪酬制度、政策及监督工作。高级管理层负责实施董事会薪酬管理方面的决议，组织制定并更新公司薪酬管理制度与政策，管理和控制薪酬预算与成本，审阅、批准员工的定薪、调薪方案、绩效考核方案、员工福利方案等。（纳入济南市市属国有企业负责人范围的公司高级管理人员，其薪酬由国资部门牵头考核确定；其他高级管理人员的薪酬由董事会提名与薪酬考核委员会和董事会根据董事会制订的薪酬考核办法考核确定。）

### 2. 年度薪酬总额、受益人与结构分布情况

2019年，公司薪酬总额为59958万元，薪酬结构分布如下：

固定薪酬19581万元，占比32.66%；可变薪酬23368万元，占比38.97%；五险一金等福利性收入17009万元，占比28.37%。

### 3. 薪酬与业绩衡量、风险调整的标准

公司总行部室员工绩效工资按照《莱商银行总行部室绩效考核管理办法》规定进行考核后兑现，分支行绩效工资按照《莱商银行绩效考核管理办法》考核后兑现。

### 4. 薪酬延期支付和非现金薪酬情况，包括因故扣回的情况。

公司建立了绩效薪酬的延期支付机制，根据员工不同层级及岗位差异，对各级管理人员以及对风险有重要影响岗位上的员工的绩效薪酬实行延期支付，并设定延期支付时间及支付比例，对延期追索及扣回的情形均做了特别的规定说明。

### 5. 薪酬政策

公司薪酬制度遵循“市场导向、岗位导向、绩效导向、能力导向”的付薪理念。于2016年开展了薪酬体系优化设计项目，根据员工岗位职责及标准进行人岗匹配，实行了以岗定薪、按月拨

付的固定薪酬制度，将薪酬总额与全行经营利润挂钩，建立自上而下的薪酬总额挂钩经营利润的提取机制，以及自下而上的薪酬总额核算机制，逐步健全全行薪酬总额管控机制。

根据监管要求并结合公司实际，公司制定了《莱商银行薪酬管理办法》《莱商银行绩效薪酬延期支付管理办法》，上述制度已经公司职工代表大会审议通过。

公司薪酬实行统一管理、分级实施的管理体系，并致力于建设科学合理的薪酬管理体系。董事会按照国家有关法律和政策确定公司的薪酬管理制度和政策，并对薪酬管理负最终责任。董事会设立提名与薪酬考核委员会，由独立董事担任委员会主任。高级管理层负责履行薪酬管理职责，总行人力资源部、计划财务部等部门负责执行薪酬政策，内审部负责对薪酬政策执行情况进行监督。

### 6. 年度薪酬方案制定及考核完成情况

公司年度薪酬方案根据人员规模、业务发展情况等制定，2019年度，公司各项经营指标良好，薪酬情况合理的反映了公司业绩及风险管理情况。

7. 超出原定薪酬方案的例外情况

报告期内，公司无超出原定薪酬的例外情况。

## 二、员工情况

报告期末，公司在职员工人数为2272人，员工中本科及以上学历1964人，占比86.44%。

#### (vi) Remunerations Structure of the Company

### 1. Remunerations Management Structure and Decision-making Procedure

The company has established a scientific remuneration management structure, clearly stipulating that the remuneration of directors and supervisors shall be decided by the general meeting of shareholders, and the Board of Directors shall decide the remuneration of senior managers. The Board of Directors is ultimately responsible for the salary management of the Bank, responsible for reviewing, approving, revising the remuneration management system and policies, and remuneration cost and budget of the Bank, supervising the implementation of the Bank's remuneration system, and regularly assessing the soundness and effectiveness of the remuneration mechanism. Under the Board of Directors is the Nomination and Remuneration Assessment Committee, which is responsible for reviewing work relating to the remuneration system, policy and supervision. Senior management is responsible for the implementation of resolutions of the Board of Directors about the remuneration management, organizing to develop and update the remuneration management system and policies, managing and controlling salary budget and cost, reviewing and approving the employees' wage fixing and salary increase plan, performance appraisal plan and staff benefit plan, etc. (The remuneration of the senior managers of the Company incorporated in the scope of the heads of state-owned enterprises in Jinan shall be determined by the department of SASAC, and the remuneration of other senior managers of the Company shall be determined following the nomination by the Board of Directors and evaluation based on the remuneration appraisal methods formulated by the remuneration appraisal committee and the Board of Directors.)

### 2. Total Annual Remuneration, Beneficiary and Structure Distribution

In 2019, the total remuneration of the Company was RMB599.58 million, with its structure distribution as follows:

RMB195.81 million of fixed compensation, accounting for 32.66% of the total amount; RMB233.68 million of variable compensation, accounting for 38.97% of the total amount; RMB170.09 million of welfare income such as Five Insurance & Two Funds ect., accounting for 28.37% of the total amount.

3. The standard of remuneration and performance measurement and risk adjustment

Performance salary of the department/office employees of the Company's head office shall be paid based on appraisal according to the provisions of the Management Measures for Performance Appraisal of Department/Office of Laishang Bank's Head Office, and that of branch employees based on the provisions of the Management Measures for Performance Appraisal of Laishang Bank.

4. Deferred payment of remunerations and non-cash remunerations, including deduction for some reason.

The Bank has established a deferred payment mechanism for performance salary and implemented deferred payment of performance salary for managers at all levels and employees in positions that have a significant impact on risks, according to different levels of employees and position differences, and set up the deferred payment time and payment ratio and made special provisions on the circumstances of deferred claims and deductions.

### 5. Policy of remuneration

The remuneration system of the Company conforms to the payment concept of the "market-oriented, post-oriented, performance-oriented, capability-oriented" principle. In 2016, the Company carried out the optimization and design program for the remuneration system, implemented a fixed remuneration system on a monthly basis according to the staff's post responsibilities and standards of person-to-job matching, linked the total remuneration to the operating profit throughout the Bank, established a top-down withdrawal mechanism with total remuneration linked to the operating profit as well as bottom-up total remuneration accounting mechanism, and gradually improved the management and control mechanism for total remuneration throughout the Bank.

In accordance with the regulatory requirements and combined with the actual situation, the Company has formulated the Measures for the Administration of Remuneration of Laishang Bank and the Measures for the Administration of Deferred Payment of Performance Remuneration of Laishang Bank, which has been deliberated and adopted by the Company's Staff Congress.

The Company's remuneration adopts uniformly managed and hierarchically implemented management system, and is dedicated to building a scientific and reasonable remuneration management system. The Board of Directors determines the Company's remuneration management system and policy according to the relevant laws and policies of the country, and is ultimately responsible for remuneration management. The Board of Directors sets up the nomination and Remuneration Assessment Committee, and independent director acts as the director of the committee. Senior management is responsible for remuneration management, and the HR Department, Financial Planning Department of the headquarter are responsible for implementing the remuneration policy and Internal Audit Department is responsible for supervising the implementation process.

6. Preparation of annual remuneration plan and completion of assessment

The Company's annual remuneration plan was prepared according to the size of staff, business development, etc. In 2019, various operational indicators of the Company were good, and the remuneration reasonably reflected the performance and risk management of the Company.

7. Exceptions beyond the original remuneration plan

There was no exception beyond the original remuneration plan in the Company during the reporting period.

## II. Profile of Employees

At the end of the reporting period, there were 2,272 employees on duty in the Company, including 1,964 with undergraduate or above degree, accounting for 86.44%.

## 第五章 公司治理结构

## 一、公司治理情况

报告期内，公司严格遵守《公司法》和《商业银行法》的有关规定，认真落实监管部门关于公司治理的有关规定，致力于进一步完善公司治理结构，增强公司治理机制的有效性，提高董事会决策的科学性。公司治理具体情况如下：

（一）关于股东与股东大会。公司按照《公司章程》和《股东大会议事规则》的要求召集、召开股东大会，积极听取股东的意见和建议，确保股东对公司重大事项的知情权、参与权和表决权。报告期内，公司召开了2018年度股东大会、2019年第一次临时股东大会，会议严格按照有关规定履行了相关法律程序，保证了股东参会并行使权利，并由律师出具了法律意见书。

（二）关于董事和董事会。公司现有董事9人，其中独立董事3人。董事会能够严格按照《公司章程》的规定，认真履行职责，在股东大会授权下进行决策，强化公司治理，推进战略步伐，促进稳健经营，维护了公司和全体股东的利益。报告期内公司共召开了6次董事会会议。

目前，公司董事会下设1个办公室和6个专门委员会，包括董事会办公室、关联交易控制委员会、风险管理委员会、战略发展委员会、审计委员会、提名与薪酬考核委员会、消费者权益保护委员会，其中风险管理委员会下设声誉风险管理专业委员会和信息科技风险管理专业委员会。6个专门委员会具有明确的职权范围，监管公司不同方面的事务，并对董事会负责，为董事会决策提供了建设性建议，进一步提高了董事会决策的科学性。

（三）关于监事和监事会。公司现有监事9人，其中职工监事3人，外部监事3人。监事会按照《公司章程》的规定认真履行职责，对公司财务、风险管理、资产质量以及部分高级管理人员履职的合规性进行监督检查，定期及不定期地与外部审计机构和管理层进行情况交流，有针对性地开展专项检查，针对风险隐患和发现的问题，向董事会和管理层提出建议和改进意见，从而更加有效地发挥监事会的作用。报告期内公司共召开6次监事会议。

（四）关于信息披露与透明度。公司在《金融时报》、公司网站发布了《2018年度报告》，并制作了《莱商银行股份有限公司2018年度报告》（摘要）单行本，及时准确地进行信息披露，切实维护广大股东特别是中小投资者的利益。

## 二、独立董事及履行职责情况

报告期内，公司董事会现有独立董事3人，独立董事本着为全体股东负责的态度，履行诚信和勤勉义务，认真参加董事会及各专门委员会会议，积极发表意见，维护公司整体利益及中小股东的合法权益不受侵犯。

（一）独立董事参加会议情况。报告期内，独立董事应参加董事会会议17人次，亲自出席15人次，委托出席2人次，缺席0人次。

（二）独立董事对公司有关事项提出异议情况。报告期内，公司独立董事未对公司本年度董事会的议案提出异议。

## 三、外部监事及履行职责情况

报告期内，公司监事会现有外部监事3人，外部监事本着为全体股东负责的态度，履行诚信和勤勉义务，认真参加监事会及各专门委员会会议，积极发表意见，维护公司整体利益及中小股东的合法权益不受侵犯。

（一）外部监事参加会议情况。报告期内，公司监事应参加监事会会议18人次，亲自出席17人次，委托出席1人次，缺席0人次。

（二）外部监事对公司有关事项提出异议情况。报告期内，公司外部监事未对公司本年度董事会、监事会的议案提出异议。

## 四、公司经营决策体系

公司最高权力机构是股东大会，通过董事会、监事会进行管理和监督。行长受聘于董事会，对公司日常经营管理全面负责。公司实行一级法人体制，各分支机构均为非独立核算单位，其经营管理活动根据公司授权进行，并对公司负责。

公司无控股股东。公司与大股东在业务、人员、资产、机构、财务五方面完全独立。公司具有独立完整的业务及自主经营能力，董事会、监事会和内部机构能够独立运作。

## 五、高级管理人员考评及激励机制

报告期内，公司董事会制定了对高级管理人员的绩效考核管理办法，建立了薪酬与公司效益和个人业绩相联系的激励约束机制；考核实行百分制，根据各高管人员的工作性质不同，考核项目有所侧重。对于非执行董事、非职工监事的考核主要以其出席会议次数及履职能力考核为主。制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会董事履职评价制度》，主要分为客观评价、自我评价和相互评价三个部分，根据各项指标具体内容的不同，给予不同的比重。公司制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会对高管人员履职评价办法》、《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对董事、监事、高管人员履职评价办法》、《关于追究董事、监事及高管人员责任的规定》。同时，公司监事会进一步量化考核标准，制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对高管人员履职评价办法实施细则》，明确了监事会对高级管理人员的履职评价内容，加大了履职问责力度。

# Chapter V Corporate Governance Structure

## 1. Corporate governance of the Company

During the reporting period, the Company strictly observed the related provisions in the Company Law and the Law on Commercial Banks, conscientiously implemented the related regulations of the regulatory intuition on corporate governance, and was dedicated to further improving corporate governance structure, enhancing the effectiveness of the corporate governance mechanism and making the decision of the Board of Directors more scientific. The performance of the Company in its corporate governance is as follows:

##### (i) Shareholders and shareholders' general meeting

The Company convened the shareholders' general meeting in accordance with the requirement of the Articles of Association and the Rules of Procedures for Shareholders' Meeting and actively listened to shareholders' opinion and suggestion to ensure that all shareholders have the right to know, to participate and to vote to the major events of the Company. During the reporting period, the Company held its 2018 annual shareholders' general meeting, and 2019 first interim shareholders' general meeting. The meetings went through related legal procedures in strict accordance with the related regulations to ensure that shareholders participate in the meetings and exercise their right. Also, legal opinion was issued by a lawyer.

##### (ii) Directors and the Board of Directors

Currently, the Company has 9 directors, including 3 independent directors. The Board of Directors is able to conscientiously perform its duties in strict accordance with the provisions in the Articles of Association, make decisions with the authorization of the shareholders' general meeting, strengthen the corporate governance, advance strategic steps and promote steady operation which safeguarded the interests of the Company and all shareholders. During the reporting period, the Company held 8 board meetings totally.

Currently, under the Board of Directors of the Company there is 1 office and 6 special committees, including the Office of Board of Directors, Related Transaction Control Committee, Risk Management Committee, Strategic Development Committee, Audit Committee, Nomination and Remuneration Assessment Committee and Consumer Rights Protection Committee. Under the Risk Management Committee are the Special Committee of Reputation Risk Management and Special Committee of IT Risk Management. These 5 special committees have specific scopes of power, supervise and manage the matters at different aspects, and are accountable to the Board of Directors. They provide constructive suggestions for the decision making of the Board of Directors further to improve the science of the decisions made by the Board of Directors.

##### (iii) Supervisors and the Board of Supervisors

The Company has 9 supervisors, including 3 employee supervisors and 3 external supervisors. The Board of Supervisors conscientiously performs its duties in strict accordance with the

provisions in the Articles of Association, carries out supervision and inspection over the compliance of the Company's finance, risk management, asset quality and the duty performance of the senior management, communicates with the external audit units and management regularly or irregularly, conducts targeted special inspections, and puts forward suggestions and improvement opinions to the Board of Directors and the management according to hidden risks or problems discovered, so as to play the role of the Board of Supervisors more effectively.

##### (iv) Information disclosure and transparency

The Company published its 2018 Annual Report on Financial Times and its website, and prepared a separate edition of 2018 Annual Report of Laishang Co., Ltd. (Summary), to carry out information disclosure in a timely and accurate manner and safeguard the interests of shareholders, particularly medium and small shareholders.

### II. Independent Directors and Their Performance of Duties

During the reporting period, there were 3 independent directors on the Board of Directors. With an attitude of being responsible to all shareholders, these independent directors performed the obligation of integrity and diligence, earnestly participated in the board meetings and meetings of various special committees, and actively delivered their opinions to safeguard the collective interest of the Company and the legitimate rights and interest of medium and small shareholders.

##### (i) Independent directors' presence in meetings

During the reporting period, the number of independent directors attending the board meetings should be 17, with presence in 15 persons, entrusted attendance in 2 persons, and absence in 0 person.

#### (ii) Objections of the independent directors to related affairs of the Company

During the reporting period, the independent directors of the Company did not object to any proposal of the Board of Directors of the current year.

### III. External Supervisors and Their Performance of Duties

There were 3 external supervisors in the Board of Supervisors in the Company during the report period. With an attitude of being responsible to all shareholders, these external supervisors performed their duty of integrity and diligence, carefully participated in the meeting of the Board of Supervisors and all special committee meetings, positively gave opinions and prevent overall benefit of the Company and lawful rights and interests of medium and small shareholders from infringement.

#### (i) Presence of external supervisors in meetings

During the reporting period, the number of the supervisors attending the Board of Supervisors Meeting should be 18, with presence in 17 persons, entrusted attendance in 1 person and absence in 0 person.

##### (ii) Objection raised by the external supervisors

During the reporting period, the external supervisors of the Company did not raise objections to the proposals of the Board of Directors and Board of Supervisors of the Company of the current year.

### IV. Operation and decision-making system of the Company

The highest authority of the Company is the Shareholders' General Meeting, which is managed and supervised through the Board of Directors and the Board of Supervisors. The president, employed by the Board of Directors, is responsible for all daily operation and management of the Company. Only the head office of the Company is its legal person, and all branches and sub-branches are non-independent accounting units, whose operation and management activities shall be carried out with the authorization of the Company and who shall be responsible for the Company.

The Company has no controlling shareholder. The Company and its majority shareholders are completely independent in terms of business, personnel, assets, organization and finance. The Company has independent and complete business and independent operation ability, and the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors and internal organizations can operate independently.

## V.Assessment and Incentive Mechanism and System of Senior Management

During the reporting period, the Board of Directors of the Company further improved the performance appraisal management methods for senior management, and established an incentive and restraint mechanism which associates remuneration with the Company's effectiveness and personal performance; A hundred-mark system for assessment; the assessment project has an emphasis based on the work nature of senior management. The assessment of non-executive directors and non-employee supervisors has mainly focused on their attendance on meetings and ability to perform their duties. The Evaluation System of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Duty Performance of Directors of the Board of Directors, which includes three parts, i.e., objective evaluation, self-evaluation and mutual evaluation, has been prepared. Different proportions are given according to different contents of various indexes. The Measures of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Assessment of Senior Management's Performance, the Measures of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Assessment of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management's Performance and the Provisions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. on Accountability of Directors, Supervisors and Senior Management have been formulated. Meanwhile, the Board of Supervisors further quantified the criteria of assessment, formulated the Implementing Rules of Duty Performance Assessment Method on Senior Management by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., specified the duty performance assessment on senior management by the Board of Supervisors and strengthened the accountability of duty performance.

## 第六章 股东大会情况

报告期内公司共召开了两次股东大会，具体情况如下：

2019年4月25日，在公司综合营业楼一楼礼堂召开了2018年度股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共51名，代表股份274,371.2120万股，占公司总股本的91.46%。大会审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司董事会2018年度工作报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会2018年度工作报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2018年度审计报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2018年度财务报告及利润分配方案》《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司2019年度预算>的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2018年度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况的报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2018年度风险管理报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2018年度消费者权益保护工作报告》《关于对〈莱商银行股份有限公司2018年度报告〉进行信息披露的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对董事2018年度履职评价报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对高级管理人员2018年度履职评价报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司监事会对监事2018年度履职评价报告》《关于对莱芜钢铁集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》《关于对齐鲁交通发展集团有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》《关于赵怀方董事申请辞职的议案》《关于2018年度核销不良贷款的议案》《关于延长第四届董监事会任期的议案》《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司三年发展规划（2019-2021）>的议案》《关于<莱商银行股份有限公司三年资本规划（2019-2021）>的议案》《关于莱商银行股份有限公司发行减记型无固定期限资本债券的议案》《关于莱芜市地鑫经贸有限公司将持有的股权转让给菏泽城建工程发展集团有限公司的议案》。

2019年10月9日，在公司综合营业楼一楼礼堂召开了2019年第一次临时股东大会，出席会议的股东及股东代理人共47名，代表股份268,130.9452万股，占公司总股本的89.38%。大会审议通过了《莱商银行股份有限公司第四届董事会工作报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司第四届监事会工作报告》《关于意向购买莱商银行综合营业楼的议案》《关于购置泰安分行营业办公楼的议案》《关于莱芜市经济开发投资有限公司及其关联企业综合授信的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年2季度内部人和股东关联交易执行情况报告》《莱商银行股份有限公司2019年2季度风险管理报告》《关于2019年度拟核销不良贷款的议案》《莱商银行股份有限公司关于发行小型微型企业贷款专项金融债券的议案》《关于选举莱商银行股份有限公司第五届董事会董事的议案》《关于选举莱商银行股份有限公司第五届董事会独立董事的议案》《关于选举莱商银行股份有限公司第五届监事会监事的议案》《关于选举莱商银行股份有限公司第五届监事会外部监事的议案》。

# Chapter VI Shareholders' General Meeting

The Company held two general meetings of shareholders during the reporting period, with the details as follows:

On April 25, 2019, the 2018 Annual Shareholders' General Meeting was held in the auditorium on the first floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 51 shareholders and authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 274,371,212 ten thousand shares, accounting for 91.46% of the Company's total share capital. The Company's Staff Congress has deliberated and adopted the 2018 Annual Work Report of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Work Report of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Audit Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Financial Report and Profit Distribution Scheme of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the 2019 Annual Budget of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Report on the Implementation of the Insider and Shareholder Related Transactions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Report on Risk Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Work Report on Consumer Rights Protection of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Information Disclosure for the 2018 Annual Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Report on Performance Evaluation of Directors by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Report on Performance Evaluation of Senior Management by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Report on Performance Evaluation of Supervisors by the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Laiwu Iron and Steel Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Qilu Transportation Development Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Application for Resignation of Director Zhao Huaifang, the Bill on the 2018 Write-off of Non-performing Loans, the Bill on Extension of the Term of the Fourth Board of Directors and Board of Supervisors, the Bill on the Three-year Development Plan (2019-2021) of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Three-year Capital Plan (2019-2021) of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Issue of Write-down Non-fixed-term Capital Bonds by Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. and the Bill on Transfer of the Equity held by Laiwu Dixin Business Co., Ltd. to Heze Urban Construction Engineering Development Group Co., Ltd.

On October 9, 2019, the First Interim Shareholders' General Meeting of 2019 was held in the meeting room on the first floor of the comprehensive business building of the Company. 47 shareholders and authorized agents attended the meeting, representing 268,130,9452 ten thousand shares, accounting for 89.38% of the Company's total share capital. The Company's Staff Congress has deliberated and adopted the Work Report of the Fourth Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Work Report of the Fourth Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Purchase Intention of the Comprehensive Office Building of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Purchase of the Office Building of Taian Branch, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Laiwu Economic Development Investment Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Report on the Implementation of Insider and Shareholder-related Transactions in the Second Quarter of 2019 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Report on Risk Management in the Second Quarter of 2019 of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Proposed Cancellation of Non-performing Loans in 2019, the Bill on the Issue of Special Financial Bonds for Small and Micro Enterprises of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Election of Directors of the Fifth Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Election of Independent Directors of the Fifth Board of Directors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on the Election of Supervisors of the Fifth Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. and the Bill on the Election of External Supervisors of the Fifth Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd.

## 第七章 董事会报告

## 一、2019年度主要经营管理情况

## （一）聚焦转型深化改革，各项业务实现了稳健、持续发展

截至2019年12月31日，公司资产总额1340.28亿元，各项存款余额957.34亿元，各项贷款余额703.54亿元，实现拨备前利润（税前）19.99亿元，不良贷款余额较年初下降0.91亿元，不良贷款率较年初下降0.43个百分点，主要指标增量和增速均居山东省内城商行前列。

## （二）规范治理机制，持续提升公司治理水平

一是规范召集召开会议，报告期内董事会共召开6次会议，审议通过议案78项，召集2次股东大会，审议议案34项。二是积极发挥各专门委员会作用，累计召开专门委员会会议20次，审议通过议案78项，增设消费者权益保护委员会，完善消费者权益保护的治理架构。三是稳妥有序完成董事会换届。严格遵循法律法规及《公司章程》有关规定，按照济南市委关于莱商银行董事会换届人选的提名，顺利完成了董事会换届各项工作。四是不断规范股权管理工作，掌握主要股东及其控股股东、实际控制人、关联方、一致行动人、最终受益人等信息，完成了主要股东履职履约评估工作，股权的规范化和精细化管理水平进一步提升。五是深化党建与公司治理的融合，全面落实党组织研究讨论是董事会决策重大问题前置程序的要求，对《董事会议事规则》进行了修改，明确了需提交党委前置研究的事项。六是真实准确的对《2018年度报告》《2019年上半年报告》进行了披露，并向山东证监局进行报备。

## （三）科学指导经营决策，充分发挥战略决策职能

一是董事会不断强化战略管理职能，认真听取了《莱商银行股份有限公司三年发展规划（2016—2018）》的评估报告，在《莱商银行股份有限公司三年发展规划（2019—2021）》的基础上，聘请上海融至道战略咨询有限公司对中长期发展战略进行进一步的优化和提升，明确未来三年的重点目标任务。二是董事会精准发力推进战略转型，有目标、有方法、有步骤的开展工作，取得了阶段性的成效。企业金融精准施策，保障了各指标及目标的有序实现，业务线上化和产品系列化取得初步成效；零售金融以产品创新为手段，提高精细化管理水平，从场景、平台、体验入手，优化流程，完善产品体系，实现了零售业务的稳健发展；贸易金融开展国际结算客户“增比扩面”活动，实现业务精准营销，创新轻资本业务并推广，上线供应链金融、跨境速汇达等产品，各项指标实现较快增长。金融市场在压降委外投资、做大投行业务、调节流动性指标、提高资金利用效率、优化理财负债结构等方面均取得实效。数字银行完成“蜂蜂贷”“蜂蜂贷（抵押）”产品的研发上线，启动了智能外呼机器人项目，积极为业务发展提供数字化技术支持。三

是增持河南方城凤裕村镇银行和山西五台莱商村镇银行股份，充分发挥了公司作为主发起行和大股东在支持村镇银行补充资本方面的作用，帮助村镇银行实现可持续稳健发展

## （四）加强全面风险管理，夯实内控合规基础

一是制定《莱商银行2019年度风险偏好策略》，合理设置资本、收益、风险等3大类35项容忍度指标，全面风险管理政策框架体系进一步完善。二是开展制度后评估工作，努力发现并解决现行制度中仍存在的制约效率、衔接配合不畅、流程设置冗杂繁复、重要环节遗漏或相互矛盾等问题，推动制定修订各类制度与格式合同145项，内控制度体系进一步完善。三是充分发挥内审作用，董事会定期听取内部审计工作报告，及时掌握内部审计在业务、管理、人员等方面发现的问题，督促整改与问责。同时坚持以业务经营为中心，以合规审计为基础，以风险审计为导向，以防风险、防案件为目标，抓住重要领域、重点业务和关键环节，年内扎实、有序开展信贷会计常规审计、金融市场专项审计、信息科技全面审计等工作，积极发挥“第三道防线”的作用，提升对风险管理的联动效能。

## （五）强化资本管理，提升资本管理精细化水平

一是董事会高度重视公司资本充足情况和变化趋势，年内以“审慎管理、目标适度、内源优先、分层补充”为基本原则，制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司三年资本规划》，对未来三年风险加权资产的增长和资本需求进行了测算，并对内源补充和外源补充的可获得性进行了分析，明确了未来三年的资本充足率管理目标。二是组织实施内部资本充足评估，从治理及政策体系、风险偏好、压力测试、资本规划等方面全面评估公司面临的各类实质性风险及其对公司资本充足状况的影响。三是加强资本约束，推进表内外资产结构调整，鼓励发展轻资本业务，降低资本消耗型资产占比，合理控制表内外风险资产增长，切实发挥资本管理对业务的支撑和引导作用。四是积极探索资本补充。在不断增强内部资本积累能力的同时，积极谋划探索运用多种资本工具的可行性，启动无固定期限资本债券发行工作，补充一级资本，以确保资本充足水平持续达标。

## （六）推进社会公益和员工关爱，持续践行社会责任

一是普惠金融精准发力，支持民营小微企业成长。公司积极围绕政策及监管变化，优化普惠金融指标体系，及时对公司普惠金融考核指标进行了优化调整，保障普惠金融业务有序发展。二是加大品牌宣传力度。将硬广与软媒有机结合，优化宣传途径。在新华社、济南日报、齐鲁晚报、大众网发布信息与宣传报道70余篇，正向引导社会舆论，提升了莱商银行社会形象。三是扎实开展消费者权益保护工作，制定了《莱商银行股份有限公司金融消费者权益保护审计办法》等制度，促进消保工作的制度化、常态化，切实维护金融消费者权益。四是以人为本，关爱员工工作生活。开展专业技术序列建设，印发《莱商银行员工职业规划管理实施细则》，开展信贷、信用风险评审条线专业技术序列建设，畅通员工晋升通道，激发员工的积极性、创造性。春节前夕开展了困难职工帮扶，划拨互助金近6万元，帮扶困难职工13名，进一步解除了员工的后顾之忧，

使职工生活多了一份保障。五是积极参与社会公益活动。2019年资助90余名贫困学生步入大学殿堂，完成学业，资助金额25.5万元。积极动员全行员工参与“春蕾计划”、慈善募捐、无偿献血等活动，践行社会责任。

## 二、董事会日常工作情况

报告期内公司董事会共召开了6次会议，具体情况如下：

## 三、2020年董事会工作计划

## (一) 工作思路

以习近平新时代中国特色社会主义思想为指导，深入贯彻十九大和十九届二中、三中、四中全会精神及中央经济工作会议精神，紧紧围绕各级党政重点工作安排，坚持高质量稳健发展总目

标，坚持稳中求进总基调，继续实施“党建统领、改革提质、创新提效、管理提速”四轮驱动，以“对公做精、零售做大、合规优先、普惠发力”为业务发展导向，努力实现全面融入济南、全面对标先进、全面转型创新、全面提升能力、全面重塑形象。

## （二）工作目标

1. 确保资本充足率达到监管要求；

2. 存款总额达到1047亿元，同比增长90亿元，增幅9.4%，存款偏离度符合监管要求；

3. 贷款总额达到784亿元，同比增长80亿元，增幅11.4%；

4. 实现净利润6.1亿元；

5.不良贷款率控制在2.5%以内，拨备覆盖率在150%以上，拨贷比达到2.5%以上；

6. 实现全年安全营运，无道德案件和重大责任案件发生。

## （三）工作重点

1. 持续完善公司治理。充分发挥三会一层作用，完善议事、考核、报告、培训机制，确保董监高依法依规履职，提高三会运作效率，确保决议得到正确贯彻执行。合理安排董事调研，更好地发挥各专门委员会和独立董事的专业支撑作用，提高董事履职能力，提升董事会决策的引领作用。进一步完善投资者关系管理，加强信息披露管理，按照监管要求完善年度报告，编制年度社会责任报告，提升经营管理的透明度，确保投资者获取信息的完整、真实、准确，维护投资者的合法权益。

2. 强力推动战略发展规划实施。优化中长期发展战略，明确未来三年的战略目标及实施步骤，从品牌建设、科技建设、队伍建设、风险管理能力、财务及资本管理等方面夯实基础，以客户为导向寻找突破点，重塑符合新战略的核心价值观，打通创新路径，促进数字化转型，实现组织的整体赋能。制定战略管理办法，构建战略管理体系，明确战略管理组织架构、职责分工及管理流程，自上而下层层推进战略解码，加强对各个层面的战略执行调研，定期进行战略评估，适时对高管层执行战略的情况出具意见和建议，确保战略实施效果。

3. 增强全面风险管理能力。持续做好信用风险、市场风险、流动性风险、合规风险等重大风险的识别和评估工作，增强风险管理的有效性，确保各项风险管理措施执行到位。完善内部控制体系，对全行各类产品、业务的流程图、风险点、控制措施进行梳理整合完善，形成统一的内控合规手册，便于各岗位人员规范掌握流程、识别与防控操作风险。建立完善的全面风险管理指标监测体系，确定各类风险的关键监测指标及阈值，定期监测、分析，全面掌握风险水平。

4. 加强资本规划和管理。进一步完善资本管理的顶层设计，保持适度的资本水平，顺应全行转型发展升级的趋势，支持业务增长和战略规划的实施，实现协调和可持续发展。强化资本内生积累，多渠道开展外源补充，增强资本实力。启动定向增资，完成20亿元无固定期限资本债券的发行，做好与主要股东、监管部门等有关各方沟通协调，全力补充一级资本。完善资本管理体系，加强对资本计量的监测，提升资本的优化配置和精细化管理水平。

5. 推动金融与科技的融合。结合商业银行数字化转型和金融科技发展趋势，制定全行金融科技应用规划，明确行内金融科技应用的方向和重点领域，设计完善金融科技应用体系架构，为下一步推动实施全行数字化转型做好顶层设计。建设统一数字化前端，整合优化APP、小程序、公众号以及其他线上获客渠道，增强客户体验。构建客户中台，为营销、考核提供全面的客户信息数据支持；构建数据中台，建设基础决策引擎、数据模型体系；构建营销中台，依托主流社交APP完善整合场景获客工具、客群经营工具，为营销工作提供系统的数字化支持。

6. 着力提升团队建设。加强薪酬政策宣讲解释，提升薪酬透明度与员工满意度；调研省会同业薪酬情况，提升薪酬福利对人才的吸引作用；通过多种渠道引进部分经验丰富的管理人员、综合素养较高的基层人员及零售经理等，适时考虑开展校园招聘，充实人才队伍，缓解基层员工断档。建立线上线下的培训学习及考核管理体系，开展中高层管理人员脱产培训，提高管理人员的战略执行能力和综合素养。调整后备干部队伍，制定后备干部管理规划，组织开展内训师大赛，加强对内训师的培养及评价考核。

7. 健全消费者权益保护体制机制。完善三会一层的消费者权益保护职责和各项规章制度，强化消费者权益保护资源配备，保证消费者权益保护工作的独立性、专业性，使消费者权益保护各项工作落到实处，真正发挥作用。细化消费者权益保护内部考核内容，加大内部考核结果的运用，确保内部考核全面覆盖，并纳入有关考评体系。

## 四、利润分配方案

2018年度计提盈余公积金46,310,359.17元，计提任意盈余公积金9,262,071.83元，提取一般风险准备304,485,251.10元；剩余未分配利润106,747,427.01元结转以后年度。

### Chapter VII Report of the Board of Directors

### I. Review of main operation and management in 2019

## (i) The Company focused on transformation and deepened the reform, and realized the steady and sustainable development in terms of all businesses.

As of December 31, 2019, the company had total assets of RMB134.028 billion, a total deposit balance of RMB95.734 billion, a total loan balance of RMB70.354 billion, a pre-provisioning profit (before tax) of RMB1.999 billion, a non-performing loan balance decreased by RMB91 million and a non-performing loan ratio decreased by 0.43 percentage points compared to the beginning of the year, with the increment of main indicators and the growth rate at the forefront of urban commercial banks in Shandong Province.

## (ii) The Company standardized the governance mechanism to continuously enhance the corporate governance level

Firstly, the Company convened and held meetings in a standardized manner. During the reporting period, the Board of Directors held 6 meetings in total, with 78 proposals reviewed and approved, and convened 2 shareholders' meetings, with 34 proposals considered. Secondly, the Company actively gave play to the role of each special committee, with a total of 20 special committee meetings held accumulatively and 78 proposals reviewed and approved. One consumer protection committee has been added to improve the governance structure for consumer rights protection. Thirdly, the Company completed the change of the term of office of the Board of Directors in a proper and orderly manner. Strictly complying with the laws and regulations and relevant provisions of the Articles of Association, the Company successfully completed the change of the term of office of the Board of Directors based on the candidate nomination of Jinan Municipal Party Committee on the change of the term of office of the Board of Directors of Laishang Bank. Fourthly, the Company continuously standardized equity management, mastered the data of major shareholders and their controlling shareholders, actual controllers, related parties, persons in concert and final beneficiaries, and completed the performance evaluation of major shareholders, thus further increasing the levels of standardization and fine management for equity. Fifthly, the Company deepened the integration between party building and corporate governance, fully implemented the requirement that research and discussion of the party organization is a pre-procedure when the Board of Directors makes major decisions, revised the Rules of Procedure of the Board of Directors and defined the matters that should be submitted to the Party Committee for pre-procedure study. Sixthly, the Company truly and accurately disclosed the 2018 Annual Report and the Report for the First Half of 2019 and filed to the Shandong Securities Regulatory Bureau for record.

## (iii) The Company scientifically guided decision-making for operation and gave full play to the function of strategic decision-making

Firstly, the Board of Directors continuously strengthened the function of strategic management, carefully listened to the evaluation report of the Three-year Development Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. (2016-2018), and hired Shanghai Financing Solutions Strategic Consulting Co., Ltd. to further optimize and improve the medium- and long-term development strategies based on the Three-year Development Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. (2019-2021), thus specifying key objectives and tasks for the next three years. Secondly, the Board of Directors made well-targeted efforts to promote strategic transformation, worked with objectives, methods and procedures, and achieved results in stages. The well-targeted implementation of corporate finance guaranteed the orderly realization of various indicators and objectives. The online business and product seriation have achieved preliminary effect; taking product innovation as a means, retail finance increased the level of fine management, optimized the process and improved the product system starting with scenarios, platforms and experiences, and realized the steady development of the retail trade; trade finance, relying on the campaign of "increasing the ratios and expanding the fields" for customers with international settlement, realized precision marketing, innovated and promoted light capital operation, and launched products such as supply chain finance and cross-border express remittance, with all indicators reversing the downward trend and achieving positive growth. The financial market has achieved substantial results in reducing external investment, increasing investment banking business, adjusting liquidity indicators, improving the capital utilization efficiency and optimizing the structure of financial liabilities, etc.. The Digital Bank has completed the R&D and launch of the "Bee Loan" and "Bee Loan (Mortgage)" products, launched the intelligent outbound robot project, and actively provided digital and technical support for businesses. Thirdly, the Company put forward proposals on increasing the shares of Fengyu Village Bank of Fangcheng, Henan and Laishang Village Bank of Wutai, Shanxi, which has fully played the role of the Company, as the main sponsor and the major shareholder, in supporting the supplementary capital of village banks, and helped village banks to achieve sustainable and steady development.

### (iv) The Company strengthened comprehensive risk management and laid a solid foundation for internal control and compliance

Firstly, the Company formulated the 2019 Risk Preference Strategy of Laishang Bank, and reasonably set up 35 tolerance indicators in 3 categories of capital, revenue and risk, further improving the framework system of comprehensive risk management policies. Secondly, the Company carried out system post-evaluation, strived to find and solve problems that still exist in the current system such as efficiency constraint, poor connection and coordination, lengthy and complicated process settings, missing or contradictory key links, and promoted the formulation and revision of 145 contracts in various types of systems and formats, further improving the internal control system. Thirdly, the Company gave full play to the role of internal audit. The Board of Directors regularly listened to the work report on internal audit, knew about problems found by the internal audit in business, management and personnel in time, and urged rectification and accountability, while taking business operation as the core, compliance audit as the basis, risk audit

as the orientation, and risk and case prevention as the goal, caught hold of key areas, key businesses and key links. The Company carried out regular audit for credit accounting, special audit for financial market and comprehensive audit for information technology in a solid and orderly manner in the year, and actively played the role of a "third line of defense" to enhance the interconnected effectiveness to risk management.

## (v) The Company strengthened capital management and enhanced the level of fine capital management

Firstly, the Board of Directors attached great importance to the Company's capital adequacy and change trend, formulated the Three-Year Capital Plan of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. and measured the growth of risk-weighted assets and capital requirement in the next three years based on the basic principle of "prudent management, appropriate objective, internal priority and layered supplementation" during the year, and analyzed the availability of internal and external supplementation to specify the goal of capital adequacy ratio management for the next three years. Secondly, the Company organized and implemented the assessment of internal capital adequacy, and comprehensively evaluated various substantial risks faced by the Company and their impacts on the Company's capital adequacy from the aspects of governance and policy system, risk preference, stress test and capital planning, etc.. Thirdly, the Company strengthened capital constraint, promoted the structural adjustment of on- and off-balance sheet assets, encouraged the development of light capital businesses, reduced the proportion of assets in the category of capital consumption, reasonably controlled the growth of on- and off-balance sheet risk assets, and practically gave play to the roles of support and guidance of capital management for businesses. Fourthly, the Company actively explored capital supplementation. While continuously enhancing the accumulation capability of internal capital, the Company actively planned to explore the feasibility of using a variety of capital instruments, started the issuance of non-fixed-term capital bonds and supplemented tier one capital to ensure that the capital adequacy level sustainably meets the standards and guarantee business development and requirements of the real economy.

## (vi) The Company promoted social welfare and employee care, and continued to practice social responsibility

Firstly, the Company made well-targeted efforts in financial inclusion to support the growth of small and micro private enterprises. The Company actively focused on policy and regulatory change to optimize the indicator system for financial inclusion. According to some new changes of the 2019 national industrial policy and regulatory requirement in the field of financial inclusion, the Company optimized and adjusted the assessment indicators for financial inclusion of the Bank in time to ensure the orderly development of the financial inclusion business. Secondly, the Company increased publicity and enhanced brand image. The Company organically combined hard advertisement with soft media to optimize the way of publicity. More than 70 information and publicity reports were published in Xinhua News Agency, Jinan Daily, Qilu Evening News and www.dzwww.com to positively lead public opinions and enhance the social image of Laishang Bank. Thirdly, the Company carried out consumer rights protection solidly and formulated the Audit

Measures of Financial Consumer Rights Protection of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd. and other systems to promote the institutionalization and normalization of consumer protection. The Company organized and implemented activities in financial consumer protection, practically protected the rights and interests of financial consumers. Fourthly, the Company put people first and cared for the work and life of employees. The Company carried out the sequential construction of professional technologies, printed and issued the Implementation Rules of Employee Career Planning Management of Laishang Bank, developed the sequential construction of professional technologies for credit and credit risk assessment lines, smoothed the promotion channels of employees, and stimulated the enthusiasm and creativity of employees. On the eve of the Spring Festival, the Company provided assistance for employees in difficulty and allocated one mutual aid fund of nearly RMB60,000 to help 13 employees in difficulty, which further relieved their worries and made them live more securely. Fifthly, the Company participated in social public benefit activities. In 2019, the Company subsidized more than 90 poor students to complete their studies in universities, with a total amount of RMB255,000. The Company actively mobilized employees of the whole bank to participate in activities such as "Spring Buds Program", and voluntary blood donation, which fulfilled the corporate social responsibility.

## III. 2020 Work Plan of the Board of Directors

##### (i) Thinking of work

Guided by Xi Jinping's Socialism Ideology with Chinese Characteristics in the New Era, thoroughly implementing the directions and keypoints discussed in the 19th National Congress of the Communist Party of China, the Second Plenary Session of the 19th Communist Party of China Central Committee, the Third Plenary Session of the 19th Communist Party of China Central Committee, the Fourth Plenary Session of the 19th Communist Party of China Central Committee, as well as the Central Economic Working Conference, and closely centered on the key work arrangements of the Party and government at all levels, the Company will hold high the general objective of "high quality and stable development", adhere to the general theme of "seeking improvement in stability", continue to implement the four-factor drive of "leadership by party construction, quality improvement by reform, efficiency increase by innovation and acceleration in management". With the business development orientation of "better public businesses, larger retail businesses, compliance as the priority, being powered by financial inclusion", The Company will make all efforts to achieve comprehensive integration into Jinan, comprehensive synchronization with advanced units, comprehensive transformation and innovation, comprehensive improvement of capabilities and comprehensive reshaping of the image.

##### (ii) Work objectives

1. Ensure that the capital adequacy ratio meets the regulatory requirements;

2. Ensure the total deposits to hit RMB104.7 billion, representing an increase of RMB9 billion or 9.4% on a year-on-year basis, with the deposit deviation complying with the regulatory requirements;

3. The total loans are planned to hit RMB78.4 billion, representing an increase of RMB8 billion or 11.4% on a year-on-year basis;

4. The net profit are planned to reach RMB 610 million;

5. The non-performing loan ratio should be controlled within 2.5%, with the provisioning coverage rate above 150% and the ratio of provisioning to loan above 2.5%;

6. Realize safe operation and no moral case and major responsibility case throughout the year.

#### (iii) Work focus

1. Continuously improve corporate governance. Give full play to the roles of the shareholder's meeting, the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors and senior management, and improve the discussion, assessment, reporting and training mechanisms to ensure that the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors and senior management perform their duties according to laws and regulations, improve the operational efficiency of the shareholder's meeting, the Board of Directors and the Board of Supervisors, and implement resolutions properly. Reasonably arrange investigations by directors, play the role of professional support from various special committees and independent directors better, improve the capability of directors to perform their duties, and enhance the leading role of the Board of Directors in making decisions. Further improve investor relations management. Strengthen information disclosure management, improve annual reports in accordance with regulatory requirements, prepare annual social responsibility reports, improve the transparency of operation management, ensure the completeness, authenticity and accuracy of information for investors, and protect the legitimate rights and interests of investors.

2. Strongly promote the implementation of strategic development plans. Optimize the medium- and long-term development strategy, specify the strategic goals and implementation procedures for the next three years, lay a solid foundation from such aspects as brand building, technology building, team building, risk management capabilities, and financial and capital management, seek customer-oriented breakthrough points, reshape the core sense of value complying with the new strategy, open up the way of innovation, promote digital transformation and realize the overall empowerment of the organization. Formulate strategic management methods, build a strategic management system, specify the organizational structure for strategic management, division of responsibilities and management processes, promote strategic resolving from top to bottom step by step, strengthen investigations for strategies at all levels, make strategic assessment regularly, and put forward opinions and suggestions on the strategy implementation of the senior management in due time to ensure the effectiveness of the strategies.

3. Enhance overall risk management capability. Continuously conduct identification and evaluation for major risks such as credit risk, market risk, liquidity risk and compliance risk, enhance the effectiveness of risk management and ensure the proper implementation of various risk management measures. Improve the internal control system, and analyze, integrate and perfect the flow charts, risk points and control measures of various products and businesses throughout the bank to form a uniform internal control compliance manual for staffs at all positions to master the process.

and identify and prevent operational risks. Establish a thorough monitoring system for comprehensive risk management indicators, determine key monitoring indicators and thresholds for various types of risks, make regular monitoring and analysis and fully understand the risk level.

4. Strengthen capital planning and management. Further improve the top-level design of capital management, maintain an appropriate level of capital, conform to the trend of transformation, development and upgrade of the whole bank, support business growth and the implementation of strategic planning, and achieve coordinated and sustainable development. Strengthen the endogenous accumulation of capital, expand external sources through multiple channels and enhance capital strength. Start a well-targeted capital increase, complete the issuance of non-fixed-term capital bonds of RMB2 billion, communicate and coordinate with major shareholders, regulatory authorities and other relevant parties, and make every effort to supplement tier one capital. Improve the capital management system, strengthen the monitoring of capital measurement and enhance the optimal configuration of capital and the level of fine management.

5. Promote the integration of finance and technology. Combining the digital transformation of commercial banks with the development trend of financial technology, formulate a financial technology application plan of the whole bank, specify the direction and key areas of financial technology application inside the bank, and design and improve the system architecture for financial technology application to make top-level design for further promoting the implementation of digital transformation throughout the bank. Build a unified digital front end, integrate and optimize APPs, applets, public accounts and other online customer acquisition channels to enhance customer experience. Build a Customer Platform to provide comprehensive customer data support for marketing and assessment; build a data platform to establish a basic decision engine and a data model system; build a marketing platform to improve and integrate customer acquisition tools and customer group operation tools based on mainstream social APPs and provide systematic digital support for marketing.

6. Focus on improving team building. Strengthen the presentation and explanation of salary policies, improve the salary transparency and employee satisfaction; investigate the salary situation of counterparties in the capital of the province, improve the attractiveness of salary and welfare to talents; introduce some experienced management personnel, grass-roots personnel with high comprehensive quality and retail managers, etc., through various channels, consider timely campus recruitment, enrich the talent team and alleviate the lack of grass-roots staffs. Establish online and offline training, learning and assessment management systems; contact with external enterprises and universities, conduct off-job trainings for middle-level and high-level management staffs to improve the strategic execution and comprehensive quality of management staffs. Adjust the team of reserve cadres, formulate management plans for reserve cadres, organize and develop competitions among internal trainers, and strengthen the training, evaluation and assessment of internal trainers.

7. Perfect the consumer rights protection system and mechanism. Improve the consumer rights protection responsibilities and various rules and regulations of the shareholder's meeting, the Board of Directors, the Board of Supervisors and senior management, strengthen the allocation of consumer rights protection resources, and ensure the independence and professionalism of consumer rights protection to put consumer rights protection into practice and make it work. Refine the internal assessment content of consumer rights protection, increase the application of internal assessment results, and ensure that internal assessment realizes full coverage and is incorporated into the relevant

assessment system.

## IV. Profit Distribution Scheme

In 2018, the surplus reserved funds withdrawn were RMB46,310,359.17, the arbitrary reserved funds withdrawn were RMB9,262,071.83 and the general risk reserves allotted were RMB304,485,251.10; the remaining undistributed profit of RMB106,747,427.01 is carried over into the later year.

### 二、监事会就有关事项发表的独立意见

### （一）公司依法经营情况

报告期内，公司坚持依法、合规经营，规范管理，经营决策程序符合法律、法规及公司章程的有关规定，董事及高级管理人员在业务经营及管理过程中谨慎、认真、勤勉，未发现违法违规行为和损害公司、股东利益行为。

## （二）财务报告真实性

2018年度财务报告已由中兴华会计师事务所根据《企业会计制度》和《企业会计准则》有关规定进行审计，并出具标准无保留意见审计报告。财务报告真实、客观、公允地反映了公司的财务状况和经营成果。

## （三）关联交易情况

公司的关联交易主要是股东贷款。监事会认为，报告期内，公司在处理关联交易时，严格遵守有关规定，履行关联交易操作程序，交易过程体现了公平合理原则，关联交易符合商业原则，未发现损害股东权益和公司利益的行为。

## （四）内部控制制度情况

监事会听取和审阅了《公司2018年度内部控制评价报告》，认为公司制定了较为完整、合理、有效的内部控制制度。

## （五）股东大会决议执行情况

监事会对董事会提交股东大会审议的各项报告和提案进行了查阅和调研，没有异议。监事会对股东大会决议的执行情况进行了监督，认为董事会按照公司治理要求，认真执行了股东大会的有关决议。

#### Chapter VIII Report of the Board of Supervisors

### 1. Meetings held by the Board of Supervisors in 2019

##### The Company held six general meetings of supervisors in 2019.

(i) On January 25, 2019, the 13th meeting of the Fourth Board of Supervisors was held, on which, the 2018 Annual Work Report of the Board of Supervisors of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Work Report on the Operation of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Work Report on Internal Audit of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Work Report on Consumer Rights Protection of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Report on the Implementation of the Insider and Shareholder Related Transactions of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the 2018 Annual Report on Risk Management of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., the Bill on Reporting the Board of Directors for Business Recording in the Fourth Quarter of 2018, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Laiwu Iron & Steel Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Comprehensive Credit of Qilu Transportation Development Group Co., Ltd. and its Associated Enterprises, the Bill on Increasing Shares and Optimizing Equity of Wutai Laishang Rural Bank Co. ## II. Independent Opinions on Relevant Issues from the Board of Supervisors

##### (i) Legal Operation of the Company

During the reporting period, the Company has stuck to operation in accordance with laws and regulations and normalization of the management. The decision-making procedure met the related provisions of laws, regulations and the Articles of Association. The directors and the senior management were able to conscientiously implement their duties. No director or senior management has been discovered to have breached any laws or regulations or Articles of Association or harmed the interest of the Company when they execute their functions.

##### (ii) Authenticity of Financial Reports

Zhongxinghua Certified Public Accountants LLP has carefully audited the 2018 Annual Financial Report in accordance with Accounting System for Enterprises and Accounting Standard for Enterprises and issued a standard unqualified audit report. The financial report has truthfully and fairly reflected the Company's financial position and operation results.

##### (iii) The Related Transactions

The related transactions of the Company are mainly shareholders' loans. The Board of Supervisors thinks that during the reporting period when dealing with related transactions, the Company has strictly observed related regulations and performed the operating procedure of related transactions. Transaction processes have reflected the principle of fairness and justifiableness. The related transactions meet the commercial principles and no action compromising the interests of the Company or shareholders has been discovered.

##### (iv) The Internal Control System

The Board of Supervisors heard and deliberated the 2018 Annual Internal Control Report of Laishang Bank Co., Ltd., and believed that the Company has formulated the relatively complete, reasonable and effective internal control system.

### (v) Implementation of the Resolutions Approved in the Shareholders' General Meetings

The Board of Supervisors has reviewed and investigated all reports and proposals submitted by the Board of Directors to the Shareholders' General Meeting for deliberation, without any objection. The Board of Supervisors has supervised over the implementation of the resolutions approved at the shareholders' general meetings, and thinks that the Board of Directors of the Company is able to conscientiously implement the related resolutions of the shareholders' general meetings according to the requirements of corporate governance.

None.
